Run log excerpt (memory-idle-growth), redacted for this repository.
Free-roam save, nobody at the controls for ~10 minutes (the title ran on its own in the open world),
main 1ef9e073, diagnostics build, -RX7900XT. Texture copies 2.7 -> 6.6 GB, card memory 9.2 -> 13.5 GB,
nothing let go for room; no plateau in 10 minutes.
Machine: AMD Radeon RX 7900 XTX 24 GB, AMD Ryzen 9 5900X, 32 GB RAM, Windows 11 Pro.
Redacted: only the layer's own counter lines are kept (never the title's output); paths, the host
name, the title's id, name, files and build hash, and console names are replaced by <...> placeholders.
Times are UTC, as in the original log.
Window: whole run- UTC; lines kept: the counters this topic needs.
Thinned: at most one line of each kind every 10 s (A/B phases, frame-time blocks and long frames all kept).
